# Test parity: every C++ case and its Rust tests

Generated by `python tools/rust/parity/parity.py --write`; do not edit. The method, the counts
per file and the verdict are in [PARITY.md](PARITY.md); the hand-reviewed rows are
`tools/rust/parity/manual.tsv`. Kinds: `exact` (the Rust name is the C++ name in snake_case),
`prefix` (the Rust name is its start), `hint` (the Rust test quotes the C++ name), `subcase`
(one Rust test per SUBCASE), `fuzzy` (the closest name in the mapped file, reviewed),
`manual` (paired by hand), `noform` (no Rust form, the reason linked), `retired` (retired by
the glue design, the replacement named). Rust paths are relative to `software_esp32_rust` and
`software_stm32_rust`.

C++ cases: 2442: manual 38, hint 12, exact 2098, prefix 16, subcase 11, fuzzy 211, noform 26, retired 30.
Rust tests: 3240, of them 788 without a C++ case.
